Extend the preceding Decode arithmetic-tiling candidate with a separate QK-norm fusion condition. For CUDA MegaMoE + FA3 + FP8 KV + BF16 H128 NeoX, normalize Q/K in registers using the existing norm::apply_norm_warp helper, retain BF16 rounding, rotate with the existing FP32 cos/sin cache, emit FP8 Q and BF16 K.
